Let's design fast score/gradient using FFT.

Definitions:
- a nonnegative length n, sum=1 for optimization.
- c = linear convolution a*a length 2n-1.
- score = 2 n * max(c) (since sum=1). Evaluator returns same ideally up to floating.
- Need minimize max(c).

Potential use cyclic convolution of length N >= 2n-1 to compute linear conv via FFT.

N = next power of two >= 2n-1 (e.g., 16384 for 4806). Memory: arrays length N ~ 8 bytes -> 134KB; fine. Multiple arrays fine.

Optimization methods in detail:

A. Smoothed objective:
F(a; τ) = max(c) - τ? We can use log-sum-exp:
U(a;τ) = max(c) + τ * log(mean? or sum exp((c_k - max)/τ)). This is smooth, approximates max. Gradient:
q_k = exp((c_k - max)/τ), Z=sum q. gradient = 2 * conv_rev(a, q/Z) truncated to n (weighted average active lags). Parent grad = 2*C where C_i = sum_m a_m w_{i?} w_R. More directly: d c_k/da_i = 2 a_{k-i} (range). Then ∇U_i = 2 ∑_k w_k a_{k-i} = 2 (a * w^{-1})? Let w_R[k?]. If we define v[i]=a_{N-1-i}? Need parent formula: w_R[k]=w[n-1-k]? They set w_R[:2n-1]=w[::-1]; Fx*FW irfft conv, C=conv[n-1:2n-1][::-1]; grad=2*C. We'll verify.

We can compute projection to simplex: update a_new = proj_simplex(a - α ∇U?). But parent normalizes after clip. For smooth objective with sum fixed, projected gradient on simplex: y=target, project onto simplex {a>=0,sum=1}. Projection via sorting O(n log n) per step, expensive but n=4806 okay? 4806 log ~ maybe 0.1ms? Python sort 4806 ~0.1ms; fine. Or use simpler multinomial gradient step (clip normalize) as parent. Projection ensures sum exactly. Clip normalize may be less stable.

Accelerated projection (FISTA) for Lipschitz function:
- Need Lipschitz L of U on simplex. c quadratic. Could estimate. Simpler backtracking line search with prox:
  At x, compute gradient g at y (interpolation y=(1-m)/(1+m)x + m z?). Projected gradient: x_new = P_Δ(x - α P_Δ(?)). Could do backtracking evaluating U at trial. But U nonconvex; line search ensure decrease.

B. Nesterov on a (not smooth globally). For nonconvex smooth U, use heavy ball/FISTA:
  y = x + (t-1)/(t+1)*δ (where δ=x - prev)
  g=grad(y)
  x_new = P_Δ(y - g/L)
  t_new=(1+sqrt(1+4t^2))/2
Need L. Could use adaptive: start with estimated L = lip*? and backtrack. But nonconvex; acceleration can overshoot. Could switch to plain near small τ.

C. Smooth max τ small (e.g., 1e-3 to 1e-4) to track exact max. But gradient noisy with many max lags. Active set exact subgradient: choose top K c_k within eps, weight uniform; gradient is average. Use line search. This may be better than logsumexp with small τ.

D. L-BFGS-B on a directly with bounded variables and objective = exact max + small regularization? Non-smooth; use smooth τ. But scipy minimize in Python may do many evals, gradient operations; we can implement our own L-BFGS? Maybe too much.

E. Use "momentum Adam" on log a. Adam could be fast but non-smooth. Need tune.

F. Use coordinate/block updates based on second-order. Could accelerate.

```
def make_obj(n, N, tau):
    def fun(x):
        xm = x - np.mean(x)
        p = np.exp(xm - xm.max()); p = p/p.sum()
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c_full = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp, N)[:2*n-1]
        cmax = c_full.max()
        shifted = (c_full - cmax)/tau
        expw = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700, 700))
        Z = expw.sum()
        U = cmax + tau*np.log(Z)
        # grad_p = 2*convrev(p, expw/Z)
        w = expw / Z
        # To compute grad_p_i = 2 sum_k w_k p_{k-i} for k-i in [0,n-1]
        # We can use FFT: define w_rev[j] = w[j]? Let's derive.
```

Implementation details for full L-BFGS:

```
def fft_convolve(a, N):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(F*F, N)[:2*len(a)-1]
```

`score_internal(p): return 2*n*np.max(conv)/ (p.sum()**2)` but p sum1. Use FFT for c if n large; for small n direct. For exact final, `evaluate_sequence`.

L-BFGS objective:
```
def make_lbfgs_obj(n,N,tau):
    Lc = 2*n-1
    def fun(x):
        xm = x - x.mean()
        # improve numerical: subtract max
        xm = xm - xm.max()  # optional? But translation invariance; subtract max changes x not used? It loses gauge, okay but p same. However x may be huge and x-mean huge; subtract max stable.
        p = np.exp(xm)
        ssum = p.sum()
        if not finite or ssum<=0... fallback uniform
        p /= ssum
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p,N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,N)[:Lc]
        # Ensure nonnegative small? c can have negative tiny due FFT; use max(0)?
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) # due roundoff; but objective should use exact nonnegative. This may slightly alter gradient? Use c positive.
        cmax = c.max()
        shifted = (c - cmax)/tau
        # clip to avoid under/overflow
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -745, 50) # since subtract max, <=0; min can be -large. exp underflows okay; no need upper.
        ew = np.exp(shifted)
        Z = ew.sum()
        U = cmax + tau*np.log(Z)
        w = ew / Z
        # grad_p
        # Need Fp_rev. We could compute p_rev FFT: np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        Fp_rev = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)  # w length Lc padded; rfft sees zero beyond Lc if w passed with len Lc? np.fft.rfft(w, N) pads.
        conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp_rev, N)
        h = conv[n-1:n-1+n]
        grad_p = 2.0 * h
        sdot = 2.0 * float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad = p * (grad_p - sdot)
        # clean nan
        if not np.isfinite(U) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
             # fallback: U=1, grad zeros
        return U, grad
    return fun
```

We compute Fp_rev every objective; but Fp_rev relates to Fp? rfft of reversed real x is not simple; need compute. Could avoid by using direct correlation via FFT of w with p? Another method: grad_p = 2 * ifft(fft(w) * conj(fft(p)))? For correlation. If we use circular correlation with padding, h_i = ∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. Cross-correlation can be computed as ifft(fft(w) * conj(fft(p))) at some offset if padded enough. Let's examine. For zero-padded length N≥L+n-1, circular correlation: corr[m] = ∑_k w[k] conj(p[(m+k) mod N])? The numpy `np.correlate(w,p)` maybe. Project simplex:
```
def proj_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, len(u)+1)
    cond = u - (cssv - s)/ind > 0
    rho = ind[cond][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho-1] - s)/rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
O(n log n) due sort; for n=4806 ~ maybe 0.2ms? Actually sorting 5k ~0.0002? Good.

```
def _score_fft(p, N=None, n=None):
   n=len(p); L=2n-1; N = N or next_pow2(max(1,2*n-1)) maybe 3n-2 for gradient? For score only N>=2n-1.
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0); return 2*n*c.max()/(p.sum()**2)
```

`_make_lbfgs_func(n,N,tau,gauge=True)`:
```
def fun(xg):
    if gauge:
       mx = max(0.0, np.max(xg) if size else 0.0)
       eg = np.exp(xg - mx)
       el = np.exp(-mx)
       ssum = eg.sum()+el
       if ssum<=0 or not finite: return 1.0, zeros
       p = np.empty(n); p[:-1]=eg/sum; p[-1]=el/sum
    else: ...
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,N)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax = c.max()
    shifted = (c-cmax)/tau
    shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0) # avoid positive? max ensures <=0 maybe round.
    ew = np.exp(shifted)  # underflow okay, no clip needed? if shifted < -745 exp=0. if shifted nan? c finite.
    Z = ew.sum()
    if Z <=0: ew[-?]=1? But cmax has at least one exp(0)=1 if max achieved; unless round cmax not in c? cmax=c.max so yes. Z>=1.
    U = cmax + tau*np.log(Z)
    w = ew/Z
    # grad_p
    Fp_rev = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp_rev, N)
    h = corr[n-1:n-1+n]
    grad_p = 2.0*h
    sdot = 2.0*float(np.dot(w, c))
    grad_full = p*(grad_p - sdot)
    if gauge:
       grad = grad_full[:-1]
    else: grad=grad_full
    # Ensure finite; if not, return large 1 and zeros.
    return float(U), grad
```

```
def optimize_fixed(p0, deadline, max_total_iters=None, tau_fracs=None, ivals=...):
   n=len(p0); N=...
   p=p0.copy(); p=p/p.sum()
   best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=score
   anchor = int(np.argmax(p))
   xg = _p_to_xg(p,anchor)
   phases = tau_fracs or [...]
   for frac in phases:
      if time> deadline-break: break
      M = max_conv(p) # compute once
      tau = max(1e-12, frac * max(M, 1e-30))
      fun = make_obj(n,N,tau,anchor)
      # estimate maxiter based on remaining; e.g. 150 for full, 30 for small
      res = minimize(fun, xg, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxls':10, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'disp':False})
      xg = res.x if finite else xg
      p_new = xg_to_p(xg, anchor)
      if p_new sum>... normalize? should sum1.
      sc = _score_fft(p_new,N)
      if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: best...
      p = p_new (even if not best? for continuation)
   # optional exact polish with active set if time
   return best_p,best_sc
```

Projected gradient function:
```
def _proj_simplex(v, s=1.0):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, len(u)+1)
    cond = u - (cssv - s)/ind > 0
    if not cond.any():
       # if all same? set uniform?
       return np.full_like(v, s/len(v))
    rho = ind[cond][-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho-1] - s)/rho
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
Test: v sum maybe >1; works. If v all negative, cond? It should find rho=1 maybe? Eg v all -1, s=1, u=-1; u - (cssv-s)/ind: i=1: -1 -(-1-1)/1=1>0; rho=1 theta=(-1-1)/1=-2; v-theta=1 -> [1,1? max?] Actually for v uniform negative, projection should uniform 1/n. Formula gives theta? Let's check v=[-1,-1], s=1, u=[-1,-1], cssv=[-1,-2]. cond i=1: -1 -(-1-1)/1 =1 >0; i=2: -1 -(-2-1)/2 = -1 +1.5=0.5>0; rho=2; theta=(cssv[1]-1)/2=(-2-1)/2=-1.5; v-theta=0.5 each. Good.
```
def optimize_fixed(p0, deadline, config):
    n=len(p0); N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    p = p0 / sum
    best_p,best_sc = score
    anchor = int(np.argmax(p))
    xg = _p_to_xg(p, anchor)

    # Variance phase
    if remaining > config.var_time:
       fun = make_variance(...); res = minimize(fun,xg,...maxiter=config.var_iter)
       xg=res.x; p=...; update
    # Max phases
    for frac, iters in config.max_phases:
       if time > deadline: break
       M = _max_conv(p,N)
       tau = max(1e-12, frac * M)
       fun=make_max(...tau)
       res=minimize(...)
       # if res.fun? update
       p=...; update
    # Small exact? maybe one variance again?
    return best_p,best_sc
```

```
def _next_pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return int(1 << (x-1).bit_length())
```

```
def _full_inits(n, parent):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None and len(parent)==n:
       p=normalize(parent); inits.append(p)
       p=(p+p[::-1])/2; inits.append(p)
       # noise small? maybe
    # param alphas
    u=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    for alpha in [-1.0,-0.75,-0.5,-0.25,0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75]:
       val = (u+1e-3)**alpha * (1-u+1e-3)**alpha
       p=val/val.sum(); inits.append(p)
    # uniform
    inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    return inits
```

```
def _score_general(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    if p.ndim!=1: p=p.ravel()
    if p.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return inf
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    n=p.size; s=float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300: return inf
    if n <= 512:
       c=np.convolve(p,p)
    else:
       N=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
       F=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(s*s))
```

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def _run_lbfgs(fun, x0, maxiter, deadline):
   maxiter=int(max(1,maxiter))
   maxfun=max(50, int(20*maxiter))
   options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8,'disp':False}
   try:
       res=minimize(fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
       x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=float)
       if x.shape != x0.shape: x=x.reshape(x0.shape)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return x0
       return x
   except Exception:
       return x0
```
If x0 size 0, minimize not called. Add in optimize.

```
def _xg_to_p(xg, n, anchor, free_idx):
   p=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
   if n==1: p[0]=1; return p
   mx=0.0
   if xg.size:
       mx=float(np.max(xg))
       if mx < 0: mx=0.0
   eg=np.exp(xg-mx) if xg.size else np.empty(0)
   ea=np.exp(-mx)
   s=eg.sum()+ea
   if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       p.fill(1.0/n); p[anchor]=max(p[anchor],1e-300); p/=p.sum()
   else:
       p[free_idx]=eg/s; p[anchor]=ea/s
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: uniform
   else: p/=s
   return p
```

```
def _p_to_xg(p,n,anchor,free_idx):
   if n==1: return np.empty(0)
   eps=np.finfo(float).tiny
   ps=np.maximum(p, eps)
   x=np.log(ps) - np.log(ps[anchor])
   return x[free_idx].astype(float, copy=False)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_p(p):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def _score_general(p) -> float:
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")

    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _score_with_N(p, N: int) -> float:
    n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if n <= 256:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _max_conv_with_N(p, N: int) -> float:
    n = int(p.size)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if n <= 256:
        c = np.convolve(p, p)
    else:
        F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    return mc / (s * s)


def _p_to_xg(p, n, anchor, free_idx):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    tiny = np.finfo(np.float64).tiny
    ps = np.maximum(p, tiny)
    x = np.log(ps) - np.log(ps[anchor])
    return np.asarray(x[free_idx], dtype=np.float64)


def _xg_to_p(xg, n, anchor, free_idx):
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1:
        p[0] = 1.0
        return p

    if xg.size == 0:
        mx = 0.0
        eg = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        mx = float(np.max(xg))
        if mx < 0.0:
            mx = 0.0
        eg = np.exp(xg - mx)

    ea = np.exp(-mx)
    ss = float(eg.sum() + ea)

    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
        p.fill(1.0 / float(n))
        p[anchor] += 1.0
        p /= p.sum()
        return p

    p[free_idx] = eg / ss
    p[anchor] = ea / ss

    ss = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
        p.fill(1.0 / float(n))
        p[anchor] += 1.0
        p /= p.sum()
    else:
        p /= ss
    return p


def _make_max_obj(n, N, tau, anchor, free_idx):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    scale = float(2.0 * n)
    tau = max(float(tau), 1e-300)

    def fun(xg):
        xg = np.asarray(xg, dtype=np.float64)
        bad_grad = np.zeros_like(xg, dtype=np.float64)

        if xg.size and not np.all(np.isfinite(xg)):
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        mx = 0.0
        if xg.size:
            mx = float(np.max(xg))
            if mx < 0.0:
                mx = 0.0
        eg = np.exp(xg - mx) if xg.size else np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        ea = np.exp(-mx)
        ss = float(eg.sum() + ea)

        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[free_idx] = eg / ss
        p[anchor] = ea / ss

        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax):
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
        shifted = np.minimum(shifted, 0.0)
        ew = np.exp(shifted)
        Z = float(ew.sum())

        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        U = cmax + tau * float(np.log(Z))
        w = ew / Z

        Fp_rev = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp_rev, N)
        h = h[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

        grad_p = 2.0 * h
        sdot = 2.0 * float(np.dot(w, c))
        grad_full = p * (grad_p - sdot)
        grad = grad_full[free_idx]

        val = float(U * scale)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            return 1e100, bad_grad
        return val, grad

    return fun


def _make_var_obj(n, N, anchor, free_idx):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    scale = float(2.0 * n)
    target = 1.0 / float(L)

    def fun(xg):
        xg = np.asarray(xg, dtype=np.float64)
        bad_grad = np.zeros_like(xg, dtype=np.float64)

        if xg.size and not np.all(np.isfinite(xg)):
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        mx = 0.0
        if xg.size:
            mx = float(np.max(xg))
            if mx < 0.0:
                mx = 0.0
        eg = np.exp(xg - mx) if xg.size else np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        ea = np.exp(-mx)
        ss = float(eg.sum() + ea)

        if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 0.0:
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[free_idx] = eg / ss
        p[anchor] = ea / ss

        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        r = c - target
        V = float(np.dot(r, r))
        if not np.isfinite(V):
            return 1e100, bad_grad

        Fr = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
        Fp_rev = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(Fr * Fp_rev, N)
        h = h[n - 1:n - 1 + n]

        grad_p = 4.0 * h
        sdot = 4.0 * float(np.dot(r, c))
        grad_full = p * (grad_p - sdot)
        grad = grad_full[free_idx]

        val = float(V * scale)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            return 1e100, bad_grad
        return val, grad

    return fun


def _run_lbfgs(fun, x0, maxiter, deadline):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
        return x0
    maxiter = int(max(1, maxiter))
    maxfun = int(max(50, 20 * maxiter))
    options = {
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 20,
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(fun, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", options=options)
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if x.shape != x0.shape:
            try:
                x = x.reshape(x0.shape)
            except Exception:
                return x0
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return x0
        return x
    except Exception:
        return x0


def _optimize_fixed_length(p0, deadline, phases, sec_per_iter):
    p = _clean_p(p0)
    if p is None:
        return p0, float("inf")

    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        q = p.copy()
        return q, _score_general(q)

    N = _next_pow2(max(1, 3 * n - 2))

    current = p.copy()
    current_sc = _score_with_N(current, N)
    best = current.copy()
    best_sc = current_sc

    anchor = int(np.argmax(current))
    all_idx = np.arange(n)
    free_idx = np.delete(all_idx, anchor)
    xg = _p_to_xg(current, n, anchor, free_idx)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(xg)):
        xg = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    for ph in phases:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 0.5:
            break
        kind = ph[0]

        if kind == "var":
            _, _, iters = ph
            min_val = 1.0
            weight = min_val
            obj = _make_var_obj(n, N, anchor, free_idx)
        else:
            _, frac, iters = ph
            M = _max_conv_with_N(current, N)
            if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
                M = 1.0
            omega = max(1e-300, float(frac) * M)
            min_val = 1.0
            weight = 1.0
            obj = _make_max_obj(n, N, omega, anchor, free_idx)

        left = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        maxit = int(min(iters, max(5, left / max(sec_per_iter, 1e-3))))
        if maxit <= 0:
            break

        xg_new = _run_lbfgs(obj, xg, maxit, deadline)
        p_new = _xg_to_p(xg_new, n, anchor, free_idx)
        p_new = _clean_p(p_new)
        if p_new is None:
            continue

        sc_new = _score_with_N(p_new, N)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(sc_new)
            best = p_new.copy()

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new <= current_sc * 1.10 + 1e-12:
            current = p_new
            current_sc = float(sc_new)
        else:
            current = best
            current_sc = float(best_sc)

        anchor = int(np.argmax(current))
        all_idx = np.arange(n)
        free_idx = np.delete(all_idx, anchor)
        xg = _p_to_xg(current, n, anchor, free_idx)

    if np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return best, float(best_sc)
    return current, current_sc


def _make_initials_length(n, parent_p):
    inits = []

    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size == n:
        p = _clean_p(parent_p)
        if p is not None:
            inits.append(p)
            ps = (p + p[::-1]) * 0.5
            ps = _clean_p(ps)
            if ps is not None:
                inits.append(ps)

    uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    inits.append(uni)

    u = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    one_minus_u = 1.0 - u + 1e-3
    u = u + 1e-3

    for a in (-1.0, -0.8, -0.6, -0.5, -0.4, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
        try:
            val = np.power(u, a) * np.power(one_minus_u, a)
            val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
            s = float(val.sum())
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                inits.append(val / s)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if n > 1:
        t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
        base = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * t)
        for b in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            try:
                val = np.power(np.maximum(base, 1e-8), b)
                val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
                s = float(val.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                    inits.append(val / s)
            except Exception:
                pass

    out = []
    seen = []
    for p in inits:
        p = _clean_p(p)
        if p is None:
            continue
        ok = True
        for q in seen:
            if np.linalg.norm(p - q) < 1e-6:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            seen.append(p)
            out.append(p)
    return out


def _update_state(p, sc, state):
    p = _clean_p(p)
    if p is None:
        return
    sc = float(sc)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state["best_sc"] - 1e-12:
        state["best_sc"] = sc
        state["best_p"] = p.copy()


def _small_param_search(deadline, state):
    lengths = [2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 96, 128]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        u = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
        um = u + 1e-3
        vm = 1.0 - u + 1e-3

        for a in (-1.2, -1.0, -0.8, -0.6, -0.5, -0.4, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2):
            try:
                val = np.power(um, a) * np.power(vm, a)
                val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
                s = float(val.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                    _update_state(val / s, _score_general(val / s), state)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if n > 1:
            t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
            base = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * t)
            for b in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
                try:
                    val = np.power(np.maximum(base, 1e-8), b)
                    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
                    s = float(val.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                        _update_state(val / s, _score_general(val / s), state)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            base2 = 1.0 - base
            for b in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
                try:
                    val = np.power(np.maximum(base2, 1e-8), b)
                    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
                    s = float(val.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                        _update_state(val / s, _score_general(val / s), state)
                except Exception:
                    pass


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    final_deadline = t_start + max(5.0, total_budget - 12.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Guaranteed valid short candidate.
    p4 = np.array([1.0 / 6.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 3.0, 1.0 / 6.0], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        sc4 = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p4]))
    except Exception:
        sc4 = _score_general(p4)
    if not np.isfinite(sc4):
        sc4 = _score_general(p4)

    state = {"best_p": p4.copy(), "best_sc": float(sc4)}

    # Use the supplied parent construction if it exists.
    parent_p = None
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                parent_p = arr / s
                _update_state(parent_p, _score_general(parent_p), state)
    except Exception:
        parent_p = None

    # If no parent is available, still have a valid answer.
    if parent_p is None:
        try:
            seq = [float(x) for x in state["best_p"]]
            sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                raise Exception
            return seq
        except Exception:
            return [float(x) for x in p4]

    # Full-length optimization around the supplied 4806-length construction.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        n_parent = int(parent_p.size)
        full_budget = 0.70 * total_budget
        full_deadline = min(final_deadline - 80.0, t_start + full_budget)

        if n_parent >= 2 and full_deadline > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
            inits = _make_initials_length(n_parent, parent_p)
            if inits:
                scored = []
                for p in inits:
                    scored.append((_score_general(p), p))
                scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

                top = [inits[0]]  # keep the parent construction itself
                for _, p in scored[1:]:
                    if len(top) >= 2:
                        break
                    if np.linalg.norm(p - top[0]) > 1e-9:
                        top.append(p)

                # Use a conservative seconds-per-iteration estimate; phases stop on deadline.
                full_phases = [
                    ("var", 0.0, 120),
                    ("max", 0.50, 60),
                    ("max", 0.25, 90),
                    ("max", 0.12, 120),
                    ("max", 0.06, 150),
                    ("max", 0.03, 180),
                    ("max", 0.015, 220),
                    ("max", 0.008, 280),
                    ("max", 0.004, 340),
                    ("max", 0.002, 400),
                ]
                sec_per_iter = 0.45

                remaining = len(top)
                for idx, p0 in enumerate(top):
                    now = time.monotonic()
                    if now > full_deadline - 10.0:
                        break
                    # Give more time to the first initialization.
                    if remaining > 1 and idx == 0:
                        phase_deadline = min(full_deadline - 20.0, now + 0.65 * (full_deadline - now))
                    else:
                        phase_deadline = full_deadline - 10.0
                    if phase_deadline <= now + 5.0:
                        break

                    p_opt, sc_opt = _optimize_fixed_length(
                        p0, phase_deadline, full_phases, sec_per_iter
                    )
                    _update_state(p_opt, sc_opt, state)
                    remaining -= 1

    # Quick finite-length parameter sweep. This is cheap and can only improve
    # the exact score if a shorter representation is better.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 20.0:
        _small_param_search(final_deadline - 15.0, state)

    # Final validation with the authoritative evaluator.
    best_p = _clean_p(state["best_p"])
    if best_p is None or len(best_p) == 0:
        best_p = p4.copy()

    seq = [float(x) for x in np.maximum(best_p, 0.0)]
    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            raise Exception
    except Exception:
        seq = [float(x) for x in p4]
        try:
            evaluate_sequence(seq)
        except Exception:
            # Ultimate fallback should still be finite and non-empty.
            seq = [1.0, 1.0, 1.0, 1.0]

    # Enforce evaluator-compatible finite nonnegative reals.
    out = []
    for x in seq:
        xf = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(xf):
            xf = 0.0
        xf = max(0.0, xf)
        xf = min(1000.0, xf)
        out.append(xf)
    if not out or sum(out) <= 0.0:
        out = [float(x) for x in p4]

    return out
```
